## Y. Telegram credential finder and shared UI conventions

These requirements come from maintainer directives while building the
"Find Telegram sessions on this computer" finder (Telegram → Enter
manually). They are written as durable conventions so later work applies
them everywhere they fit instead of rediscovering them per screen. Each
applies **uniformly** across every applicable surface of the app.

| ID     | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | State                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| R-Y1   | The finder lists only credentials proven live: a token or session Telegram actively rejects is dropped; one that cannot be checked (network/timeout) is kept, marked unverified.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done: `verifyRecord`/`isDeadError` in `js/src/sources/auto-import/telegram-creds.js`; `scan` filters `active !== false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| R-Y2   | Credentials are deduplicated by account and collapsed to one connectable entry per type (login by session, bot token, login by phone & password) — never one row per file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Done: `dedupeRecords` + `groupRecords`/`mergeRecords`; tested in `js/tests/telegram-creds.test.js`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| R-Y3   | User accounts are listed before bot accounts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Done: `groupRecords` stable-sorts by account kind (user rank 0, bot rank 1); test "user accounts are listed before bot accounts".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| R-Y4   | Every place a credential was found is kept; the shortest path is shown first and the remaining locations expand behind a "+N more" / "Show less" toggle.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Done: finder `byLength` sorts `wheres`; `WhereLine` in `js/src/web/connections/telegram-sessions.js` is an expandable list.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| R-Y5   | Masked hints reveal at most the first three and last three characters of a token/session (short values fall back to a tail-only mask); raw secret values never leave the machine or reach logs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Done: `maskEnds`/`maskHint`; BigInt-safe JSON everywhere; values are never returned to the browser or logged.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| R-Y6   | Connecting a credential always gives explicit visible feedback (a "Connected" mark) — a successful click is never silent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done: `connected` state in `useFinder`; the entry replaces its button with a check-marked "Connected" badge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| R-Y7   | Verification states ("confirmed with Telegram") render as a single small check-mark icon whose words live in a tooltip, via the shared `VerifiedMark`, wherever a confirmed credential is shown. The tooltip is a styled, reliable CSS tooltip (`data-tooltip`, shown on hover and keyboard focus), not the native `title` (slow and easy to miss); an `aria-label` keeps the words for screen readers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Done: `VerifiedMark` + the `check` icon in `js/src/web/icons.js`; | R-Y8   | Persistent chrome (the top menu bar) stays above transient overlays: the tutorial spotlight ring and its dimming clip at the header's bottom edge and never float over the top menu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Done: `.topbar.glass` sits at `z-index: 2001`, above the `z-index: 2000` tutorial overlay; no tutorial step targets a header control.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| R-Y9   | Verbose server logs are on by default in the app folder, capturing every request and every error with its stack, so any failure's root cause is already on disk.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Done: `createServerLog`/`installCrashLogging` write `<store>/logs/meta-sovereign.log`; `--quiet-logs` opts out.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| R-Y10  | Generated artefacts (e.g. `CHANGELOG.md`) are excluded from the copy-paste duplication gate, so release automation cannot trip it; the gate measures authored source only.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Done: `**/CHANGELOG.md` is in `.jscpd.json` `ignore`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| R-Y11  | The tutorial reacts to real progress: finishing a connection (a saved session, a phone login, a bot token or API keys) advances past the "connect a service" step, however the user connected — the tour never sits on a step the user already completed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Done: connect paths dispatch `meta-sovereign:connected` (`announceConnected`); the `setup` step's `completeOnConnect` advances on it (`useCompleteOnConnect`); `TUTORIAL_VERSION` bumped to 4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| R-Y12  | Connecting Telegram makes it work, not just stores a key: the connected account's chats, recent messages and address-book contacts appear in Chat and Contacts without another step, and Chat never asks to connect a service that is already connected.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Done: `js/src/sources/telegram-account.js` (`createTelegramAccount`) syncs a user session over MTProto (dialogs, messages, contacts) into `msg:telegram:*` / `person:telegram:*` links; a sync starts after `use` and after the phone login, on server start, and every 30 s for a user session (only changed dialogs are re-read); `GET /api/telegram/status`, `POST /api/telegram/sync`; the empty Chat shows the account and sync state (`ChatAccountEmpty`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| R-Y13  | Messages typed in Chat to a Telegram chat are sent through the connected account (a user session as the user, a bot token as the bot), and are stored once under the network's message id.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Done: the server's `telegram` adapter posts through the account (`accountSources`); `handleSend` stores the account's canonical link; a bot token is never polled in the background (`getUpdates` would take its updates).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| R-Y14  | Contacts lists the people the user can talk to, including address-book contacts not written to yet, with a search and a "Message" action that opens a chat with the person; the tutorial shows Contacts and then how to open a chat from there.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Done: `aggregateContacts(links, { people: true })` merges `person:` links with `open`; `ContactsView` adds search and "Message" (`#chat=<source>:<chat>`, an empty chat named from the contact, history loaded on open via `POST /api/telegram/history`); tutorial steps `contacts` → `contacts-open` → `chat`, `TUTORIAL_VERSION` 5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| R-Y15  | A message is sent exactly once: Send empties the box at once, so pressing Enter twice or clicking again sends nothing more; a retried or repeated request for the same message never posts it twice; messages of one chat go out in the order sent; every chat keeps its own unsent draft; Enter that confirms an input-method word is not a Send.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Done: the composer gives each message a `clientId` and queues it per chat (`js/src/web/chat-live.js`); `/api/send` sends one `clientId` at most once and shares the outcome with repeats (`sendOnce`); Telegram gets a `random_id` derived from it (`RANDOM_ID_DUPLICATE` = already sent), plain text, split at 4096 characters (`js/src/sources/telegram-send.js`); an account error never falls back to sending through the browser (`fallback: false`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| R-Y16  | Each of the user's messages shows its delivery like a messenger: a clock while sending, ✓ when sent, ✓✓ when read by the other side, and a failed one says why (wait time for flood limits, no right to write, unknown chat) with Retry and Delete.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Done: `deliveryStatus` / `chatMessages(links, key, { pending })` in `chat-model.js`; read receipts are `read:telegram:<chat>` links (`outboxMaxId`) from the dialogs and from `UpdateReadHistoryOutbox`; Telegram errors map to codes in `sendError`; `Delivery` / `FailedActions` in `chat-view.js`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| R-Y17  | New messages, edits, deletions, read receipts and typing from the connected Telegram account appear in the open chat as they happen, without reloading; the view stays where the user is reading and offers "New messages ↓" instead of jumping. Settings → Connection type chooses Direct, SOCKS5 (host, port, optional user and password) or MTProto proxy (host, port, secret). The setting is stored beside the Telegram session's other secrets. The password and secret are write-only: the page only learns that they are set, and a saved one is kept only for the same host. Saving a changed proxy reconnects at once. The key exchange follows Telegram's protocol (phone.requestCall, acceptCall, confirmCall, with the g_a hash, DH and fingerprint checks); the key never leaves the server. The four key emoji come from SHA256(key ‖ g_a) and Telegram's 333-emoji table, which is protocol data taken from TDLib under the Boost Software License 1.0. The microphone is requested only on Accept or Call. A second ring during a call is answered busy; an unanswered call ends as missed; calls left open by a restart are ended. | R-Y222 | Communities no longer break Telegram reads. Found in the logs of the user's real account: since layer 232 Telegram lists a community or communityForbidden chat among the chats of its answers, and GramJS throws "Cannot cast Community to any kind of int" on them; the periodic dialog sync failed and the one-time re-read of unsupported messages failed on 3 chats, leaving 16 messages unsupported. Now, as GramJS reads an answer whose constructor has a chats vector (found from the schema text: dialogs, messages, differences, updates, resolved peers and the like), the community entries are dropped before anything uses them; the existing skip of peerless dialogs shares the same wrapper. Communities stay unshown. The re-read repair (and the forward-name repair) now log why reads failed, as telegram_repair_unsupported.reasons, a count per short code (the error code, the Telegram error name, or the first 40 characters of the message with digits removed); never chat ids, names or text. Tested with fakes and the real GramJS classes offline (a serialised answer read back through BinaryReader); the symptom was seen on the real account's logs, the fix is not yet checked against real Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `js/src/sources/telegram-layer.js`, `js/src/sources/telegram-repair.js`, `js/tests/telegram-community.test.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| R-Y224 | Files, video and audio inside rich messages and Instant View pages can be downloaded and played. A file row has a Download link that saves the file under its own name; a video or audio block is a player in the page (preload none, no autoplay) that seeks by byte range. Files are downloaded through the Telegram client (upload.getFile) only after a click or play, up to 64 MB (413 beyond), from the rich message's stored documents or the Instant View page's documents; the server route sanitises the saved name (control characters, quotes and slashes removed, UTF-8 filename*) and serves anything that is not a video or audio as an attachment. | R-Z2   | The store survives crashes and shared use. Writes are grouped and flushed to disk within a 200 ms group-commit window: a power cut may lose up to the last 200 ms of writes but never corrupts the store, and on macOS fsync does not force the drive cache, so the guarantee is the OS-level one. One process writes a store at a time (`data.lock`): a second writer such as `import` while `serve` runs fails fast with STORE_LOCKED and a clear message, while read-only commands (`export`, `cv-read`) take no lock and never write. Unreadable lines are quarantined to a side file instead of being dropped or breaking startup; the live data.lino/data.bin format is unchanged. Store files are created 0600 and folders 0700, and older ones are tightened on start. Plain (unencrypted) backups exclude `secret:*` links and say so; encrypted backups include them; old backups are pruned only when the newest one verifies. Logs redact tokens, Authorization/Cookie headers, sessions and passwords, and rotate by size (10 MB, 3 files, 0600). Limitations: without `MS_SECRET_PASSPHRASE` secrets are plain text in `data.lino`; a lock left by a dead process is taken over, but a lock on a network filesystem is not guaranteed. Not checked against real Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `js/src/storage/durable.js`, `js/src/storage/store-lock.js`, `js/src/storage/lino-store.js`, `js/src/storage/dual-store.js`, `js/src/storage/backup.js`, `js/src/server/redact.js`, `js/src/server/log.js`, `js/tests/store-durability.test.js`, `js/tests/redact-log.test.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| R-Z14  | The app survives a full disk. (1) A store write the disk refuses is rejected with STORE_WRITE_FAILED, kept in memory (up to 64 MB) and retried with growing waits (5 s up to 60 s); the store is then degraded, and past the limit new writes are refused with STORE_FULL; when the disk takes the data again the queue is written in order and the store recovers, and flush() never answers while data is only queued. (2) The logger never throws: lines it cannot write are counted and one log_lines_dropped line follows when writing works again, and the crash handlers cannot fail while reporting. (3) A free-space guard skips the snapshot, compaction and backup with reason low_disk instead of half-writing them; the backup route answers 507 with code LOW_DISK. (4) Temporary files left by a failed write are removed. (5) /api/health reports the free disk space (or null when the platform cannot say) and the store state (degraded, queued bytes). (6) The page shows a dismissible banner "Disk almost full" under 1 GiB free or while writes fail, and none when the free space is unknown. (7) Telegram: the account counter and each channel counter never move past an update whose store write failed; the next catch-up fetches it again. The store's media folder and its files are now private (0700/0600) like logs and archives. Limit: a live Telegram update whose store write fails more than about 1 s after it arrived can be lost if the server crashes in that window. Not checked against real Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `js/src/storage/write-queue.js`, `js/src/storage/lino-store.js`, `js/src/storage/disk-space.js`, `js/src/storage/dual-store.js`, `js/src/storage/backup.js`, `js/src/server/log.js`, `js/src/server/routes-backup.js`, `js/src/server/routes-derived.js`, `js/src/sources/telegram-catchup.js`, `js/src/sources/telegram-channels.js`, `js/src/sources/telegram-account-live.js`, `js/src/web/disk-banner.js`, `js/tests/disk-full.test.js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Every automated send (automation runner, autopilot, greetings, re-engage sessions, broadcast and outreach, both broadcast routes and the broadcast handler) goes through one `guardedSend` outbox. The key is automation id + chat + UTC day; a `sending` record is written before the send (if that write fails with `STORE_WRITE_FAILED` or `STORE_FULL`, nothing is sent: `outbox-unavailable`) and `sent`, `failed` or `unknown` after. A timeout, `send-unknown`, `unknown-delivery` or an ambiguous error is `unknown`, never `failed`; an unknown send is never retried, and at startup `recoverOutbox` turns a leftover `sending` into `unknown`, which the user settles on the page as arrived or not. Greetings are paced (30 s with +-25% jitter), capped at 50 per day and 20 per request, and stop after 3 failures in a row or on any rate limit or unknown send; a dry run lists who would get what and sends nothing. Nothing is sent on startup. The Automations page has a "Send safety" panel (quiet hours, opt-out list, unknown sends). Secrets are masked in the outbox. Known limits: greetings are still sent within one request (capped at 20-50); the planner's skip flags depend on adapters storing them; personal WhatsApp greetings go through the Cloud API and fail clearly without its credentials. Crash safety without copying the store: the log length (with the inode, the line ending and the last bytes) goes to `migration.json` first, a migration only appends, and on failure or after a crash the next open cuts the log back to that length (and drops `data.bin`); if the log is shorter than recorded or is not the file the journal describes, nothing is cut and `MIGRATION_FAILED` says so. | R-Z11  | GitHub as a full inbox. Every pulled item keeps `externalId` (kind, REST id, node id, owner, repo, number) and `messageId`, so edit (`PATCH` the issue, PR or comment), delete and react (`POST .../reactions`) go to the right endpoint and never guess a target; items without an id refuse with `no-external-id` (409), and migration 0004 backfills old items from their stored url / raw. Replies pick the issue-comments endpoint or the review-comment replies endpoint. Review comments keep path, line, side, commit and diff hunk. The notifications inbox (`GET /notifications`) uses `since`, `If-Modified-Since` and `X-Poll-Interval`; it marks read only through the explicit `POST /api/github/notifications/read`. Pulls are incremental per repository (a cursor saved after the store write, ETags, 304s are free), follow Link-header pagination everywhere, and handle rate limits (`retry-after`, `x-ratelimit-reset`): a short wait is slept, a long one returns `rate-limited` with `retryAfter`, remembered so nothing loops. Discussions are read and answered through GraphQL. The GitHub card has a repository picker, toggles for notifications and Discussions, a schedule (off until repositories are picked) and Fetch now; both run as the `github` job of the shared pull runner (`POST /api/pull`), started only after the server listens and only with a saved token. Limits: no cross-repository search inbox, no OAuth device flow, notifications need a classic token. Message ids are collision-free (Facebook: thread folder, millisecond, sender digest and counter; LinkedIn: conversation, ISO time, sender digest and counter), so same-moment messages and a second import never merge or double. Facebook/Instagram latin-1 mojibake is repaired only when the bytes round-trip; photos, videos, audio, files and stickers are copied to `media/<network>/<sha256>.<ext>` (files 0600, folders 0700); reactions, participants, group type, unsent flag, thread id from the folder and `message_N.json` parts are merged in order; the HTML export is read; an Instagram export uses the Facebook recipe. X imports one-to-one and group DMs, the `YTD` prefix, media and names from `account.js`; LinkedIn keeps ids, participants, folders and CSV quoting. Dates that name no zone (HTML export, LinkedIn) are read in the machine's time zone. A ZIP is read as a file with a streaming reader (ZIP64 included), never loaded whole; one broken file is an error row and the rest import; a failing store or disk write stops the import with its code; batched `putMany`. Migration 0005-archive-ids moves old Facebook/LinkedIn ids (old ids stay resolvable through aliases), writes Facebook times as ISO text, and the importer finds an old-scheme message by its old id or alias so a re-import adds no duplicates. | R-Z16  | Backups and restores work on a multi-gigabyte store. A backup is now a streamed, gzip-compressed `meta-sovereign-<time>.msbk` (`.msbk.enc` when encrypted: AES-256-GCM per frame, scrypt key), format 2: a manifest line (format, createdAt, secrets, source, `schema`), the store's log records one per line, and a trailer with the line count and SHA-256. It is read from `data.lino` in chunks and written to a temp file, fsynced and renamed, so memory stays flat whatever the size and no half-written file looks complete; the archive is read back and compared before it is reported. A plain backup leaves `secret:*` out. Restore checks the whole file first (a changed, cut or reordered byte is refused), then replays records 500 at a time; `restore --replace` (`restoreBackupToDir`) swaps the whole log in one step. The manifest carries the data schema (R-Z17): a backup from a newer version is refused with `BACKUP_TOO_NEW`, one from an older version restores and the store migrates on its next open; old `.json`/`.json.enc` backups still restore. The automatic backup is now ON by default: every 24 hours (`MS_BACKUP_INTERVAL_HOURS`, `0` = off), the newest 3 kept (`MS_BACKUP_KEEP`, `0` = keep all, never deleting the newest good one), plain unless a passphrase is set, 0600 in a 0700 folder, skipped with a log line on a nearly full disk. No schema migration is needed (stored data is unchanged). For the owner of a channel or supergroup, Edit has Channel type (Group type): Public or Private, and for Public a t.me link checked as it is typed once typing pauses (channels.checkUsername) and saved only with the button (channels.updateUsername; an empty name makes the chat private); when Telegram says the user has too many public channels (CHANNELS_ADMIN_PUBLIC_TOO_MUCH) those channels are listed with Revoke link (channels.getAdminedPublicChannels, then channels.updateUsername with an empty name); where collectible usernames exist, Deactivate all usernames asks again before channels.deactivateAllUsernames. Whoever may change the info of a channel gets Appearance: name and profile colour with a background emoji (channels.updateColor) and the channel's emoji status (channels.updateEmojiStatus), Telegram's boosts-required answer told in words. The owner of a channel or group gets Delete channel (Delete group): a dialog names the chat and says it cannot be undone; Escape closes only that dialog, and the confirmation sends channels.deleteChannel or, for a basic group, messages.deleteChat (the chat then leaves the list like a left one). When joining a channel by an invite link or from a similar-channels preview is refused for the limit (CHANNELS_TOO_MUCH), a dialog lists the user's inactive channels (channels.getInactiveChannels) with checkboxes; Leave (channels.leaveChannel) leaves only the ticked ones that Telegram lists as inactive. Nothing is written until a button is pressed. Already-read candidates are skipped, including on Next. A failed check shows its reason and retries instead of inventing an empty queue. ---

## Traceability

When a future PR or commit lands a feature, reference the requirement
ID in the changeset and PR description (e.g. _"R-A3: doublets+lino
dual-store implementation"_) so this list stays a live spec rather than a
snapshot.

### Conversation reliability and familiar message interaction

- Operator requires a positive unread count and validates a Telegram peer with a read-only dialog lookup before opening it. Zero-count manual marks do not qualify. Failed directory loads or availability probes never fabricate an empty queue or a logged-out account. Last-known data, in-flight directory reads and the live stream are shared per account.
- Chat, folder, title and Operator badges use documented count units and scope; Chat notifications exclude muted chats, while Operator counts conversations across folders. Tooltips expose the full count behind compact badges.
- Chat and embedded Operator cards support full-line message selection and context menus, including outside the centered bubble column. Existing text selection, links, media, range selection, touch gestures and keyboard actions remain usable.
- Earlier history loads automatically based on scrolling. The idle saved-message notice and manual earlier-history button are removed in both views; active loading and waits stay visible.
- Reply suggestions deduplicate both sources and reject results for stale text/chat. An empty composer may show relevant replies. Telegram Business bot banners require a real bot identity and are distinct from explicit active/paused/off app autopilots.

Evidence: `js/tests/chat-feed.test.js`, `js/tests/telegram-dialog-state.test.js`, `js/tests/live-subset.test.js`, `js/tests/hosted-ui.test.js`, `js/tests/operator.test.js`, `js/tests/reply-cache.test.js`, `js/tests/telegram-business.test.js`, `js/tests/e2e-operator-browser.mjs`, `js/tests/e2e-chat-selection-browser.mjs`, and `js/tests/e2e-chat-loading-browser.mjs`.
